# Improvements catalog

The single list behind the "1000 optimizations, improvements, fixes" effort.
Base: `main` @ `37aeb70`. Every row was found by one agent and then
adversarially re-checked by another against the code at that base (the
checker tried to refute it; refuted, not-meaningful, duplicate and
product-sized findings were dropped and are not listed).

Sources: `bl#N` - [backlog.md](backlog.md) item, `au#T7`/`au#B5` -
[audit.md](audit.md) top-50 / below-the-cut, `rec#E12` - recommendation.md
Track E, `lint:<names>` - clippy pedantic/nursery site judged meaningful,
`sw:` / `sw2:` / `sw3:` - fresh sweep rounds 1-3.

## Counting rule

An item counts only when `M` is `y` **and** its status is `done:<sha>`:

- one item = one mechanism in one function;
- a lint fix counts only if it changes behavior or guards an invariant
  (checked conversion, clamp/saturate, shorter lock scope, a clone removed
  from a hot path); style-only lint cleanups are never counted;
- a regression test is part of its bug item; a `test` item counts only for
  an edge case that had no test;
- a refactor counts only if it removes duplication or creates a tested unit;
- anything already fixed before this effort never counts.

Count: `grep -E '^\| I-[0-9]{4} ' improvements.md | awk -F'|' '$6~/y/ && $11~/done:/' | wc -l`

Columns: `Sev` high/med/low (a trailing `?` = the checker found it plausible
but could not fully prove the trigger); `Size` S <=20 changed lines, M <=80,
L <=200; `PR` the planned bucket; `Status` open / wip / done:`<sha>` /
refuted (disproved while implementing) / deferred / proposal.

PR buckets: P1 correctness bugs; P2 panics and numeric safety; P3
concurrency and lock scope; P4 performance; P5 error handling and feedback;
P6 small UX polish; P7 missing tests and real refactors; P8 durability core
(transfer, journal, persistence, fs_at, native copy, version store,
workspace delete/fileops/transfer queue), test-first.
